context = ref<UserCustomerContextView | null>(null)\nconst loading = ref(false)\nconst initialized = ref(false)\n\nexport function useAuth() {\n  const { apiFetch } = useApi()\n  const snackbar = useSnackbar()\n\n  const isAuthenticated = computed(() => !!context.value?.user_id)\n  const isAdmin = computed(() => !!context.value?.is_customer_admin)\n  const isSystemAdmin = computed(() => (context.value?.user_record?.system_user_mode ?? 0) >= 2)\n  const userCommEnabled = computed(() =>\n    !!(context.value?.settings as Record<string, unknown> | aiProvidersConfigured = computed(() =>\n    !!(context.value?.settings as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.ai_providers_configured,\n  )\n  const userName = computed(() => context.value?.name || '')\n  const customerName = computed(() => context.value?.customer_name || '')\n  const trialDays = computed(() => context.value?.trial_expiration_days ?? null)\n  const stripeCallbackPrefix = computed(() => context.value?.stripe_callback_prefix || '')\n\n  async function login(email: string, password: string, twoFactorCode?: string) {\n    loading.value = true\n    try {\n      const result = await apiFetch<UserCustomerContextView & Login2FAChallenge>('/auth-ws/login-and-get-context', {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: {\n          email,\n          password,\n          two_factor_code: twoFactorCode || undefined,\n        },\n      })\n      if (result.two_factor_required) {\n        return result\n      }\n      if (result.failure_reason) {\n        throw new ApiError(result.failure_reason, 400, result)\n      }\n      context.value = result\n      initialized.value = true\n      return result\n    }\n    finally {\n      loading.value = false\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function loginPlain(email: string, password: string, twoFactorCode?: string) {\n    loading.value = true\n    try {\n      const result = await apiFetch<LoginResponse>('/auth-ws/login', {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: {\n          email,\n          password,\n          two_factor_code: twoFactorCode || undefined,\n        },\n      })\n      if (result.two_factor_required) {\n        return result\n      }\n      await refreshContext()\n      return result\n    }\n    finally {\n      loading.value = false\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function logout() {\n    try {\n      await apiFetch<{ message?: string }>('/auth-ws/logout', { method: 'GET' })\n    }\n    catch {\n      // still clear local session\n    }\n    context.value = null\n    initialized.value = true\n    await navigateTo('/login')\n  }\n\n  async function refreshContext(includeAccountStatus = false) {\n    loading.value = true\n    try {\n      const result = await apiFetch<UserCustomerContextView>('/ws/core/get-user-customer-context', {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: { include_account_status: includeAccountStatus },\n      })\n      if (result.failure_reason) {\n        context.value = null\n        return null\n      }\n      context.value = result\n      return result\n    }\n    catch {\n      context.value = null\n      return null\n    }\n    finally {\n      loading.value = false\n      initialized.value = true\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function ensureSession() {\n    if (initialized.value) return isAuthenticated.value\n    await refreshContext()\n    return isAuthenticated.value\n  }\n\n  async function signup(payload: CustomerSelfRegPayload) {\n    return apiFetch<CustomerUserCreateResult>('/auth-ws/initial-signup', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: payload,\n    })\n  }\n\n  async function confirmSignup(activationCode: string) {\n    return apiFetch<CustomerUserCreateResult>(`/auth-ws/signup-confirm/${encodeURIComponent(activationCode)}`, {\n      method: 'GET',\n    })\n  }\n\n  async function requestPasswordReset(email: string) {\n    return apiFetch<string>('/auth-ws/password-reset-request', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: { email },\n    })\n  }\n\n  async function changePasswordViaToken(token: string, newPassword: string, email?: string) {\n    return apiFetch<boolean>('/auth-ws/change-password-via-token', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: {\n        token,\n        new_password: newPassword,\n        email: email || undefined,\n      },\n    })\n  }\n\n  function requireAdmin() {\n    if (!isAdmin.value) {\n      snackbar.error('Admin access required for this action')\n      return false\n    }\n    return true\n  }\n\n  function requireSystemAdmin() {\n    if (!isSystemAdmin.value) {\n      snackbar.error('System admin access required for this action')\n      return false\n    }\n    return true\n  }\n\n  return {\n    context,\n    loading,\n    initialized,\n    isAuthenticated,\n    isAdmin,\n    isSystemAdmin,\n    userCommEnabled,\n    aiFeaturesEnabled,\n    aiProvidersConfigured,\n    userName,\n    customerName,\n    trialDays,\n    stripeCallbackPrefix,\n    login,\n    loginPlain,\n    logout,\n    refreshContext,\n    ensureSession,\n    signup,\n    confirmSignup,\n }\n}","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":207,"totalLines":207,"truncated":false}},"title":"client/composables/useAuth.ts","time":{"start":1787409613659,"end":1787409613975}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a029ea34c37692bf477cdd6bf7ce17"}},"id":"prt_029ea36bd001vkH7UASs74E9Bx","sessionID":"ses_fd6165f6fffeKuWeoV2bgaMkui","messageID":"msg_029ea2ea2002i8FRpJVy7pvZ3O"}}

project_tag: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Optional filter limiting scoped sections to one project tag\")\n    application_tag: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Deprecated alias for project_tag\")\n\n\nclass ApiKeyCountByTag(BaseModel):\n    application_tag: str\n    total: int = 0\n    active: int = 0\n\n\nclass DashboardSummary(BaseModel):\n    project_count: int = 0\n    project_active_count: int = 0\n    application_count: int = 0\n    application_active_count: int = 0\n    api_key_count: int = 0\n    api_key_active_count: int = 0\n    team_user_count: int = 0\n    api_key_count_by_tag: list[ApiKeyCountByTag] = Field(default_factory=list)\n\n\nclass ProjectDashboardRow(BaseModel):\n    id: int\n    project_tag: str\n    project_title: str | None = None\n    project_description: str | None = None\n    disabled: bool = False\n    create_date: datetime | None = None\n    modify_date: datetime | None = None\n\n\nclass ApplicationDashboardRow(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Compatibility alias shape for older clients.\"\"\"\n    id: int\n    application_tag: str\n    application_description: str | None = None\n    disabled: bool = False\n    create_date: datetime | None = None\n    modify_date: datetime | None = None\n\n\nclass ApiKeyDashboardRow(BaseModel):\n    id: int\n    description: str | None = None\n    application_tag: str\n    active_status: bool = True\n    has_webhook: bool = False\n    create_date: datetime | None = None\n\n\nclass PrimaryAccountStatus(BaseModel):\n    customer_id: int\n    customer_name: str | None = None\n    customer_email: str | None = None\n    account_status: str | None = None\n    customer_activated: bool = False\n    stripe_customer_id: str | None = None\n    trial_expiration_date: datetime | None = None\n    service_expiration_date: datetime | None = None\n    trial_expiration_days: int | None = None\n    sandbox_count: int | None = 0\n\n\nclass DashboardEntriesView(WsResultPackage):\n    process_time: float = Field(default=0.0, description=\"The time taken to process the request in seconds\")\n    generated_at: datetime | None = Field(default=None, description=\"UTC timestamp when the snapshot was assembled\")\n    summary: DashboardSummary = Field(default_factory=DashboardSummary)\n    projects_summary: list[ProjectDashboardRow] = Field(default_factory=list)\n    applications_summary: list[ApplicationDashboardRow] = Field(default_factory=list)\n    api_keys_summary: list[ApiKeyDashboardRow] = Field(default_factory=list)\n    account_status: PrimaryAccountStatus | None = None\n\n\ndef _utcnow() -> datetime:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)\n\n\ndef _as_naive(dt: datetime | None) -> datetime | None:\n    if dt is None:\n        return None\n    if dt.tzinfo is not None:\n        return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)\n    return dt\n\n\ndef _enum_value(value) -> str | None:\n    if value is None:\n        return None\n    return value.value if hasattr(value, \"value\") else str(value)\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/core/get-dashboard-information\",\n    summary=\"Return package of entries for quick dashboard views\",\n    description=(\n        \"Returns a dashboard snapshot: project and API key counts, \"\n        \"project/API key summaries, and primary account status.\"\n    ),\n    tags=[\"dashboard\"],\n)\ndef get_dashboard_info(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n    dashboard_request: RequestDashboardEntries,\n) -> DashboardEntriesView:\n    return get_dashboard_info_sync(\n        session=session,\n        usr_context=usr_context,\n        dashboard_request=dashboard_request,\n    )\n\n\ndef get_dashboard_info_sync(\n    session: Session,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n    dashboard_request: RequestDashboardEntries,\n) -> DashboardEntriesView:\n    start_request_time = time.time()\n    now = _utcnow()\n    customer_id = usr_context.sess_user.customer_id\n    limit = dashboard_request.recent_limit\n    project_tag = dashboard_request.project_tag or dashboard_request.application_tag\n    project_tag = project_tag if project_tag not in (None, \"\", \"string\") else None\n\n    result = DashboardEntriesView(generated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc))\n\n    customer = usr_context.sess_customer\n    if customer is None:\n        customer = session.exec(select(Customer).where(Customer.id == customer_id).limit(1)).one_or_none()\n        usr_context.sess_customer = customer\n\n    if customer is not None:\n        trial_expiration_days = None\n        if customer.customer_account in (None, \"\") and customer.trial_expiration_date is not None:\n            trial_exp = _as_naive(customer.trial_expiration_date)\n            if trial_exp is not None:\n                days_left = (trial_exp - now).days\n                if days_left >= 0:\n                    trial_expiration_days = days_left\n        result.account_status = PrimaryAccountStatus(\n            customer_id=customer.id,\n            customer_name=customer.customer_name,\n            customer_email=customer.customer_email,\n            account_status=_enum_value(customer.account_status),\n            customer_activated=bool(customer.customer_activated),\n            stripe_customer_id=customer.customer_account,\n            trial_expiration_date=customer.trial_expiration_date,\n            service_expiration_date=customer.service_expiration_date,\n            trial_expiration_days=trial_expiration_days,\n            sandbox_count=customer.sandbox_count or 0,\n        )\n\n    project_query = select(CustomerProject).where(\n        CustomerProject.customer_id == customer_id,\n        CustomerProject.deleted_date == None,  # noqa: E711\n    )\n    if project_tag is not None:\n        project_query = project_query.where(CustomerProject.project_tag == project_tag)\n    projects = list(session.exec(project_query.order_by(CustomerProject.project_tag)).all())\n\n    key_base = [ApiAccessConfiguration.customer_id == customer_id]\n    if project_tag is not None:\n        key_base.append(ApiAccessConfiguration.application_tag == project_tag)\n\n    api_keys = list(\n        session.exec(\n            select(ApiAccessConfiguration)\n            .where(*key_base)\n            .order_by(desc(ApiAccessConfiguration.create_date))\n            .limit(limit)\n        ).all()\n    )\n    result.api_keys_summary = [\n        ApiKeyDashboardRow(\n            id=k.id,\n            description=k.description,\n            application_tag=k.application_tag,\n            active_status=bool(k.active_status),\n            has_webhook=bool(k.webhook_endpoint),\n            create_date=k.create_date,\n        )\n        for k in api_keys\n    ]\n\n    key_count_total = session.exec(\n        select(func.count()).select_from(ApiAccessConfiguration).where(*key_base)\n    ).one()\n    key_count_active = session.exec(\n        select(func.count()).select_from(ApiAccessConfiguration).where(\n            *key_base, ApiAccessConfiguration.active_status == True  # noqa: E712\n        )\n    ).one()\n    key_tag_rows = session.exec(\n        select(\n            ApiAccessConfiguration.application_tag,\n            func.count().label(\"total\"),\n            func.sum(case((ApiAccessConfiguration.active_status == True, 1), else_=0)).label(\"active\"),  # noqa: E712\n        )\n        .where(*key_base)\n        .group_by(ApiAccessConfiguration.application_tag)\n    ).all()\n    api_key_count_by_tag = [\n        ApiKeyCountByTag(application_tag=tag or \"\", total=int(total or 0), active=int(active or 0))\n        for tag, total, active in key_tag_rows\n    ]\n\n    team_user_count = session.exec(\n        select(func.count()).select_from(User).where(\n            User.customer_id == customer_id,\n            User.deleted_date == None,  # noqa: E711\n        )\n    ).one()\n\n    project_active_count = sum(1 for p in projects if not p.disabled)\n\n    project_rows: list[ProjectDashboardRow] = []\n    app_compat_rows: list[ApplicationDashboardRow] = []\n    for project in projects:\n        project_rows.append(\n            ProjectDashboardRow(\n                id=project.id,\n                project_tag=project.project_tag,\n                project_title=project.project_title,\n                project_description=project.project_description,\n                disabled=bool(project.disabled),\n                create_date=project.create_date,\n                modify_date=project.modify_date,\n            )\n        )\n        app_compat_rows.append(\n            ApplicationDashboardRow(\n                id=project.id,\n create_date=project.create_date,\n                modify_date=project.modify_date,\n            )\n        )\n    project_rows.sort(key=lambda r: r.project_tag or \"\")\n    app_compat_rows.sort(key=lambda r: r.application_tag or \"\")\n    result.projects_summary = project_rows[: max(limit, 5)]\n    result.applications_summary = app_compat_rows[: max(limit, 5)]\n\n    count = len(projects)\n    result.summary = DashboardSummary(\n        project_count=count,\n        project_active_count=project_active_count,\n        application_count=count,\n        application_active_count=project_active_count,\n        api_key_count=int(key_count_total or 0),\n        api_key_active_count=int(key_count_active or 0),\n        team_user_count=int(team_user_count or 0),\n        api_key_count_by_tag=api_key_count_by_tag,\n    )\n\n    result.process_time = time.time() - start_request_time\n    return \"\"\"pvf_app_runner — the standard, executable application runner for pvf-based applications.\n2: \n3: Startup orchestration:\n4:   1. load pvf_app_startup.yaml (bindings, feature switches, share vocabularies)\n5:   2. ingest the configured env file (usually .env) with python-dotenv\n6:   3. inject startup-owned values into pvf_settings (read-only thereafter)\n7:   4. establish the database connection FIRST; on failure, clear diagnostics to stderr\n8:      and exit with an error code\n9:   5. register pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n10:   6. build the four FastAPI applications (noauth / session / share / external) and the\n11:      root app; register pvf-owned routers\n12:   7. invoke the application shell entry point with a PvfInvocation; it registers hooks\n13:      and routers and returns None (success), an error message, or raises\n14:   8. complete setup: include application routers, merge OpenAPI tag metadata, run the\n15:      dependency-injection integrity check, install handlers, mount apps and static files\n16: \n17: Run with:  fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py   (or fastapi run ... for production)\n18: The module-level `app` is the composed ASGI applications\n49: MOUNT_PREFIXES = {\"noauth\": \"/auth-ws\", \"session\": \"/ws\", \"share\": \"/ext-ws\", \"external\": \"/api\"}\n50: \n51: # Non-app paths (reserved prefixes) always return JSON 404s rather than the SPA page\n52: RESERVED_PREFIXES = [\"/ws\", \"/ext-ws\", \"/api\", \"/auth\", \"/hook-stripe-events\", \"/api/webhooks\"]\n53: \n54: EXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG = 2\n55: EXIT_DATABASE_FAILED = 3\n56: EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED = 4\n57: \n58: # The composed applications, populated by build_app(); consumed by tooling\n59: # (OpenAPI snapshot extraction, diagnostics).\n60: {\"name\": \"self-reg\", \"description\": \"Self Registration Services for Initial Signup\"},\n66:     ],\n67:     \"session\": [\n68:         {\"name\": \"admin\", \"description\": \"Services to re.sub(r\"://([^:@/]+):([^@/]+)@\", r\"://\\1:****@\", dsn)\n101:         _fatal(\n102:             \"could not establish a connection to the database.\\n\"\n103:             f\"  DSN: '{name}' across composed apps\")\n127:             merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"][name] = schema\n128:         seen_tags = {t[\"name\"] for t in merged[\"tags\"]}\n129:         for tag in .bindings.pvf_startup_config import (\n    PvfStartupConfig,\n    PvfStartupConfigError,\n    apply_startup_config,\n    load_startup_config,\n)\n\n_WORKSPACE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]  # pvf -> src -> workspace root\n_STATIC_DIR = str(_WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"static\")\n_STATIC_CLIENT_DIR = str(_WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"static_client\")\n\n# Mount prefixes for the four FastAPI applications\nMOUNT_PREFIXES = {\"noauth\": \"/auth-ws\", \"session\": \"/ws\", \"share\": \"/ext-ws\", \"external\": \"/api\"}\n\n# Non-app paths (reserved prefixes) always return JSON 404s rather than the SPA page\nRESERVED_PREFIXES = [\"/ws\", \"/ext-ws\", \"/api\", \"/auth\", \"/hook-stripe-events\", \"/api/webhooks\"]\n\nEXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG = 2\nEXIT_DATABASE_FAILED = 3\nEXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED = 4\n\n# The composed applications, populated by build_app(); consumed by tooling\n# (OpenAPI snapshot extraction, diagnostics).\ncomposed_apps: dict[str, FastAPI] = {}\n\n_PVF_TAGS = {\n    \"noauth\": [\n        {\"name\": \"auth\", \"description\": \"Services to support authentication\"},\n        {\"name\": \"self-reg\", \"description\": \"Self Registration Services for Initial Signup\"},\n    ],\n    \"session\": [\n        {\"name\": \"admin\", \"description\": \"Services to customer/user administration and customer/user information\"},\n        {\"name\": \"context\", \"description\": \"Services to access the user/customer context and related user information\"},\n        {\"name\": \"devops\", \"description\": \"Services to access and post log events for diagnostic purposes\"},\n    ],\n    \"share\": [],\n    \"external\": [\n        {\"name\": \"access_check\", \"description\": \"Signed API connectivity checks\"},\n    ],\n}\n\n\ndef _fatal(message: str, exit_code: int) -> None:\n    print(f\"FATAL: {message}\", file=sys.stderr)\n    raise SystemExit(exit_code)\n\n\ndef _load_env_file(startup_config: PvfStartupConfig) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Ingest the configured env file (usually .env). Process environment wins.\"\"\"\n    from dotenv import load_dotenv\n\n    env_path = Path(startup_config.env_file)\n    if not env_path.is_absolute():\n        env_path = _WORKSPACE_ROOT / env_path\n    if env_path.is_file():\n        load_dotenv(env_path, override=False)\n\n\ndef _probe_database_or_exit(db, dsn: str) -> None:\n    try:\n        with db.get_engine().connect() as connection:\n            connection.execute(text(\"SELECT 1\"))\n    except Exception as ex:\n        masked_dsn = re.sub(r\"://([^:@/]+):([^@/]+)@\", r\"://\\1:****@\", dsn)\n        _fatal(\n            \"could not establish a connection to the database.\\n\"\n            f\"  DSN: {masked_dsn}\\n\"\n            f\"  error: {type(ex).__name__}: {ex}\",\n            EXIT_DATABASE_FAILED,\n        )\n\n\ndef _merge_openapi_specs(title: str, specs: list[tuple[str, FastAPI]]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge (mount_prefix, app) OpenAPI specs into one schema; paths are prefixed.\n\n    Component name collisions are tolerated only when the schemas are identical\n    (shared pvf models); conflicting definitions are a build error.\n    \"\"\"\n    merged: dict | None = None\n    for prefix, sub_app in specs:\n        spec = sub_app.openapi()\n        if merged is None:\n            merged = {\"openapi\": spec[\"openapi\"], \"info\": {\"title\": title, \"version\": spec[\"info\"].get(\"version\", \"\")},\n                      \"paths\": {}, \"components\": {\"schemas\": {}}, \"tags\": []}\n        for path, item in spec.get(\"paths\", {}).items():\n            merged[\"paths\"][prefix + path] = item\n        for name, schema in spec.get(\"components\", {}).get(\"schemas\", {}).items():\n            existing = merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"].get(name)\n            if existing is not None and existing != schema:\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"Conflicting OpenAPI schema definitions for '{name}' across composed apps\")\n            merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"][name] = schema\n        seen_tags = {t[\"name\"] for t in merged[\"tags\"]}\n        for tag in spec.get(\"tags\", []):\n            if tag[\"name\"] not in seen_tags:\n                merged[\"tags\"].append(tag)\n                seen_tags.add(tag[\"name\"])\n    return merged or {}\n\n\n_sessions_openapi_cache: dict = {}\n\n\ndef build_sessions_openapi() -> dict:\n    \"\"\"The aggregate session-facing contract: root + noauth + session + share apps.\"\"\"\n    if not _sessions_openapi_cache:\n        specs = [(\"\", composed_apps[\"root\"])]\n        for target in (\"noauth\", \"session\", \"share\"):\n            specs.append((MOUNT_PREFIXES[target], composed_apps[target]))\n        _sessions_openapi_cache.update(_merge_openapi_specs(\"Session UI API\", specs))\n    return _sessions_openapi_cache\n\n\ndef build_external_openapi() -> dict:\n    return composed_apps[\"external\"].openapi()\n\n\ndef build_app(config_path: str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    global composed_apps\n\n    # 1. startup YAML\n    try:\n        startup_config = load_startup_config(config_path)\n    except PvfStartupConfigError as ex:\n        _fatal(str(ex), EXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG)\n\n    # 2. env file (python-dotenv; process environment overrides file values)\n    _load_env_file(startup_config)\n\n    # 3. settings + read-only startup value injection\n    from .config.pvf_config_settings import pvf_settings\n\n    apply_startup_config(startup_config, pvf_settings)\n    pvf_settings.process_name = os.path.basename(__file__)\n\n    if not pvf_settings.UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK:\n        _fatal(\n            \"This system appears to be running on an example or missing configuration file; \"\n            \"check the .env contents (UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK must be 1).\",\n            EXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG,\n        )\n\n    # 4. database connection first\n    from .db.connect import DatabaseConnection\n\n    db = DatabaseConnection()\n    _probe_database_or_exit(db, pvf_settings.DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING)\n\n    # 4b. application schema extensions must be declared before any model import:\n    # import the shell module (declarations only; no models at module level by\n    # contract) and let it register added columns on pvf tables\n    try:\n        shell_module = importlib.import_module(startup_config.application.shell_module)\n    except Exception as ex:\n        _fatal(\n            f\"could not import application shell module '{startup_config.application.shell_module}': {ex}\",\n            EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED,\n        )\n    from .db.model_factory import get_schema_extension_registry\n\n    register_schema_extensions = getattr(shell_module, \"register_schema_extensions\", None)\n    if callable(register_schema_extensions):\n        register_schema_extensions(get_schema_extension_registry())\n\n    # 5. pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n    from .db.models import pvf_bootstrap\n\n    pvf_bootstrap.import_core_models()\n    pvf_bootstrap.import_feature_models(\n        activate_stripe_integration=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION,\n        activate_branding_image_store=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE,\n        activate_share_links=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS,\n        activate_external_api=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API,\n        activate_customer_communication=pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION,\n    )\n\n    # 6. build the FastAPI applications\n    from .bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfAppTarget, PvfInvocation, set_current_invocation\n    from .utils.fastapi_app_handlers import (\n        handle_500_internal_error,\n        handle_422_unprocessable_entity,\n        make_spa_fallback_404_handler,\n    )\n\n    # per-app 404 defaults to JSON; the root app gets the SPA fallback below\n    sub_app_exceptions: dict = {}\n    if pvf_settings.PYTEST_ACTIVE is False:\n        # don't handle 500 errors while testing, so complete error message is visible in pytest results\n        sub_app_exceptions[500] = handle_500_internal_error\n\n    def _make_sub_app(title: str, target: str) -> FastAPI:\n        sub = FastAPI(title=title, openapi_tags=list(_PVF_TAGS[target]), exception_handlers=dict(sub_app_exceptions))\n\n        @sub.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)\n        async def _handle_422(request, exc):\n            return await handle_422_unprocessable_entity(request, exc)\n\n        return sub\n\n    app_noauth = _make_sub_app(f\"{pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Entry API\", \"noauth\")\n    app_session = _make_sub_app(f\"{pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Session UI API\", \"session\")\n    app_share = _make_sub_app(f\"{pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Share Link API\", \"share\")\n    app_external = _make_sub_app(f\"{pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Customer API\", \"external\")\n\n    for browser_app in (app_noauth, app_session, app_share):\n        browser_app.add_middleware(\n            CORSMiddleware,\n            allow_origins=[\"http://localhost:3000\", pvf_settings.APPLICATION_BASE_URL],\n            allow_credentials=True,\n            allow_methods=[\"*\"],\n            allow_headers=[\"*\"],\n            expose_headers=[\"content-disposition\"],\n        )\n\n    # public liveness probe on the external app (exempt from the signed-header check)\n    from fastapi import APIRouter\n\n    external_minimal_router = APIRouter()\n\n    @external_minimal_router.get(\"/status\")\n    async def external_status():\n        return {\"service\": f\"{pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Service Customer Facing API\",\n                \"version\": pvf_settings.PVF_VERSION,\n                \"status\": \"API Available\"}\n\n    app_external.include_router(external_minimal_router)\n\n    # pvf-owned routers\n    from .api.auth import router as auth_router\n    from .api.log_events_request import router as log_events_request_router\n    from .api.user_requests import noauth_router as self_reg_router, router as user_requests_router\n\n    app_noauth.include_router(auth_router)\n    app_noauth.include_router(self_reg_router)\n    app_session.include_router(user_requests_router)\n    app_session.include_router(log_events_request_router)\n\n    root_exceptions = dict(sub_app_exceptions)\n    root_exceptions[404] = make_spa_fallback_404_handler(_STATIC_CLIENT_DIR, RESERVED_PREFIXES)\n    root_app = FastAPI(title=f\"{pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Service\", docs_url=\"/root-docs\", redoc_url=None,\n                       openapi_tags=[{\"name\": \"stripe-event-hook\", \"description\": \"Capture, log, and process updates from stripe via webhook\"}],\n                       exception_handlers=root_exceptions)\n\n    @root_app.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)\n    async def _root_handle_422(request, exc):\n        return await handle_422_unprocessable_entity(request, exc)\n\n    # SPA host routes (/alive, /)\n    from .api.hello_web import router as web_files_router\n\n    root_app.include_router(web_files_router)\n\n    # 7. application entry point (shell module imported above, before model registration)\n    invocation = PvfInvocation(\n        pvf_settings=pvf_settings,\n        startup_config=startup_config,\n        db=db,\n        app_noauth=app_noauth,\n        app_session=app_session,\n        app_share=app_share,\n        app_external=app_external,\n    )\n    set_current_invocation(invocation)\n\n    entry_point = getattr(shell_module, startup_config.application.entry_point, None)\n    if not callable(entry_point):\n        _fatal(\n            f\"application shell '{startup_config.application.shell_module}' has no callable \"\n            f\"'{startup_config.application.entry_point}'\",\n            EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED,\n        )\n\n    try:\n        startup_result = entry_point(invocation)\n    except SystemExit:\n        raise\n    except Exception as ex:\n        _fatal(\n            f\"application startup raised {type(ex).__name__}: {ex}\\n{format_exc()}\",\n            EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED,\n        )\n    if startup_result is not None:\n        _fatal(f\"application startup failed: {startup_result}\", EXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED)\n\n    from .api.app_context_views import (\n        bind_client_session_context_settings,\n        noauth_router as context_noauth_router,\n        router as context_router,\n    )\n    from .api.app_post_subscriber_transactions import router as subscriber_tx_router\n\n    if invocation.client_session_context_settings_type is not None:\n        bind_client_session_context_settings(invocation.client_session_context_settings_type)\n    app_noauth.include_router(context_noauth_router)\n    app_session.include_router(context_router)\n    app_session.include_router(subscriber_tx_router)\n\n    if pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION:\n        from .api.hook_stripe_events import router as hook_stripe_events_router\n        root_app.include_router(hook_stripe_events_router)\n\n    if pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE:\n        from .api.app_branding import router as branding_router, share_router as branding_share_router\n        app_session.include_router(branding_router)\n        app_share.include_router(branding_share_router)\n\n    if pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS:\n        from .api.share_link_manage import router as share_link_manage_router\n        app_session.include_router(share_link_manage_router)\n\n    if pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API:\n        from .api.api_access_management import router as api_access_management_router\n        from .api.api_probe import router as api_probe_router\n        app_session.include_router(api_access_management_router)\n        app_external.include_router(api_probe_router)\n\n    if pvf_settings.ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION:\n        from .api.app_user_communication import router as user_communication_router\n        app_session.include_router(user_communication_router)\n        \n    # include application routers and merge tag metadata\n    for target in PvfAppTarget:\n        target_app = invocation.target_app(target)\n        for registration in invocation.routers[target]:\n            target_app.include_router(registration.router)\n        target_app.openapi_tags.extend(invocation.openapi_tags[target])\n\n    # 8. dependency-injection integrity (strict, regardless of endpoint parameters)\n    from .utils.dependency_integrity import verify_app_dependency_integrity\n\n    verify_app_dependency_integrity(app_noauth, \"noauth\")\n    verify_app_dependency_integrity(app_session, \"session\")\n    verify_app_dependency_integrity(app_share, \"share\")\n    verify_app_dependency_integrity(app_external, \"external\")\n\n    # root status (reports the application's identity/version when supplied by the shell)\n    @root_app.get(\"/status\")\n    async def root_status():\n        return {\"service\": f\"{invocation.application_name or pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Service UI API\",\n                \"version\": invocation.application_version or pvf_settings.PVF_VERSION,\n                \"status\": \"API Available\"}\n\n    # aggregate session-surface contract (root + noauth + session + share)\n    @root_app.get(\"/session/docs.json\", include_in_schema=False)\n    async def sessions_openapi():\n        return JSONResponse(build_sessions_openapi())\n\n    @root_app.get(\"/session/docs\", include_in_schema=False)\n    async def sessions_docs():\n        return get_swagger_ui_html(openapi_url=\"/session/docs.json\", title=\"Session UI API - Docs\")\n\n    # mounts: API prefixes first, static last\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"external\"], app_external, name=\"external_api\")\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"noauth\"], app_noauth, name=\"noauth_api\")\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"session\"], app_session, name=\"session_api\")\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"share\"], app_share, name=\"share_api\")\n\n    root_app.mount(\"/static\", StaticFiles(directory=_STATIC_DIR), name=\"static\")\n    root_app.mount(\"/\", StaticFiles(directory=_STATIC_CLIENT_DIR), name=\"static_client_files\")\n\n    composed_apps = {\n        \"root\": root_app,\n        \"noauth\": app_noauth,\n        \"session\": app_session,\n        \"share\": app_share,\n        \"external\": app_external,\n    }\n\n    from .utils.utils_show import show_vars_semi\n\n    show_vars_semi(\"pvf_app_runner is running\", app_name=pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME, pvf_vers=pvf_settings.PVF_VERSION, pid=os.getpid())\n    return root_app\n\n\napp = build_app()","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":410,"totalLines":410,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py","time":{"start":1787409646703,"end":1787409646724}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a029eabfc273a3b27ea95dcd71ac49"}},"id":"prt_029eabfe9001HCVjLOvRZfcr92","sessionID":"ses_fd6165f6fffeKuWeoV2bgaMkui","messageID":"msg_029eab5fd002ub0qqZyLuKzqj0"}}

bind_client_session_context_settings() before OpenAPI.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _parse_settings_blob(raw) -> dict:\n    if raw in (None, \"\", \"null\"):\n        return {}\n    if isinstance(raw, dict):\n        return raw\n    if isinstance(raw, str):\n        try:\n            data = json.loads(raw)\n            return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}\n        except Exception:\n            return {}\n    return {}\n\n\ndef _client_settings_defaults():\n    inv = invocation_context\n    if inv is not None and inv.app_client_settings is not None:\n        return copy.deepcopy(inv.app_client_settings)\n    settings_type = inv.client_session_context_settings_type if inv is not None else None\n    if settings_type is not None:\n        return settings_type()\n    return PvfClientAuthSettings()\n\n\ndef _user_communication_available() -> bool:\n    return bool(getattr(runtime_settings(), \"ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION\", False))\n\n\ndef _ai_yaml_activated() -> bool:\n    return bool(getattr(runtime_settings(), \"ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS\", False))\n\n\ndef _ai_features_available() -> bool:\n    return _ai_yaml_activated()\n\n\ndef = str(getattr(customer, \"ai_provider\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n        key = decrypt_secret(getattr(customer, \"ai_api_key\", None) or \"\").strip()\n        return bool(provider) and _customer_llm_configured(customer)\n\n\ndef _merged_client_settings(*blobs):\n    merged = _client_settings_defaults()\n    for raw in blobs:\n        data = _parse_settings_blob(raw)\n        for k, v in data.items():\n            if hasattr(merged, k):\n                setattr(merged, k, v)\n    if hasattr(merged, \"enable_user_communication\"):\n        merged.enable_user_communication = Field(default=-1, description=\"system assigned id when customer was created\")\n    customer_name: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"customer's business name\")\n    customer_email: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"provided email for the customer level\")\n    stripe_customer_id: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Indicates customer number associated with period\")\n    stripe_callback_prefix: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Prefix for stripe callback URLs associated with the customer\")\n    stripe_callback_prefix_sandbox: str | None = masked_fields.append(extra)\n        if not masked_fields:\n            return data\n        for record_key in (\"user_record\", \"customer_record\"):\n            record = data.get(record_key)\n       check_trial_expiration_days = (check_expiration_date - datetime.now(timezone.utc)).days\n        if check_trial_expiration_days >= 0:\n            trial_expiration_days = cur_customer.customer_name\n    context_view.customer_email = cur_customer.customer_email\n    context_view.settings = _merged_client_settings(\n        cur_customer.client_settings,\n        usr_context.sess_user.client_settings,\n    )\n    if hasattr(context_view.settings, \"ai_providers_configured\"):\n        context_view.settings.ai_providers_configured = _ai_providers_configured(cur_customer)\n    apply_2fa_client_settings(context_view.settings, usr_context.sess_user, cur_customer)\n\n    account_users_check = User.get_account_users(session=session, usr_context=usr_context)\n    if account_users_check.failure_reason != '':\n        context_view.failure_reason = account_users_check.failure_reason\n        context_view.log_id = account_users_check.log_id\n        return context_view\n\n    context_view.team_info = []\n    for account_user_row in account_users_check.user_info_list:\n None:\n        return CustomerAiSettingsResult(failure_reason=\"Customer not found\")\n    provider = str(form.ai_provider or \"\").strip()\n    if provider:\n        provider = customer.ai_provider = provider\n    else:\n        customer.ai_provider = None\n    customer.ai_model = (str(form.ai_model or \"\").strip() or None)\n    customer.apply_ai_api_key_update(form.ai_api_key)\n    customer.update_customer_system(session=session, clear_lock=False)\n    return _ai_settings_view(customer)\n\n\n@router.post(\n TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=denied)\n    customer = _load_session_customer(session, usr_context)\n    if customer is None:\n        return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=\"Customer not found\")\n    provider = normalize_llm_provider(form.ai_provider)\n    if not provider_is_supported(provider):\n        return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=\"Unsupported LLM provider\")\n    key = _resolve_submitted_key(customer, form.ai_api_key)\n    if not key:\n        return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(failure_reason=NO_PROVIDERS_CONFIGURED)\n    verified = verify_llm_provider(provider, key, form.ai_model)\n    return TestCustomerAiConnectionResult(\n        ok=bool(verified.get(\"ok\")),\n        models=list(verified.get(\"models\") or []),\n        provider=verified.get(\"provider\") or provider,\n        failure_reason=\"\" if verified.get(\"ok\") else (verified.get(\"error\") or \"Provider authorization failed\"),\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/core/llm-available-models\",\n    summary=\"Admin: list models from the resolved customer or system LLM provider\",\n    tags=[\"context\"],\n)\nasync def llm_available_models(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n) -> LlmAvailableModelsResult:\n    if not _ai_yaml_activated():\n        return LlmAvailableModelsResult(failure_reason=\"AI features are not enabled\")\n    denied = _require_customer_admin(usr_context)\n    if denied:\n        return LlmAvailableModelsResult(failure_reason=denied)\n    customer = _load_session_customer(session, usr_context)\n    if customer is None:\n        return system_llm_configured():\n        s = runtime_settings()\n        provider = normalize_llm_provider(getattr(s, \"AI_SERVICE\", \"opencode_go\"))\n        key = str(getattr(s, \"AI_API_KEY\", \"\") or {"type":"text","timestamp":1787409745794,"sessionID":"ses_fd6165f6fffeKuWeoV2bgaMkui","part":{"id":"prt_029eb9181001c090NY58J99idu","messageID":"msg_029eb419e0026KEIYjddZBC5v6","sessionID":"ses_fd6165f6fffeKuWeoV2bgaMkui","type":"text","text":"**Execution Plan — Node `client-version-in-context-to-client-14n3y0` (FAST TRACK)**\n\n**Overall:** Expose server `active version` (`src/config/config_settings.py:13` `VERSION=0.7.80` via `pvf_app_runner.py:375` `/status` / invocation.application_version) and client build version in the context payload delivered to browser, compare on frontend, render dismissible yellow banner with `Reload` → `window.location.reload()`.\n\n**Summary Scope:** Backend: add version fields to `UserCustomerContextView` (`src/pvf/api/app_context_views.py:141`) populated from `runtime_settings()`/ `invocation_context`. Frontend (Nuxt3 SPA `client/nuxt.config.ts:16`, `client/app.vue:1`, `client/layouts/default.vue:91`): inject `APP_VERSION`, compare, banner. No new versioning logic.\n\n**Numbered Implementation Steps**\n\n1. **Backend — Add versions to context view:** Extend `src/pvf/api/app_context_views.py:141` `UserCustomerContextView` with `server_version: str|None` and `client_expected_version: str|None` (or `active_version`/`client_version`). Alternative: reuse `settings` blob but explicit fields preferred for typed client (`client/types/api.ts:59`). Populate in `get_user_customer_context()` `src/pvf/api/app_context_views.py:192` from `runtime_settings().VERSION` or `pvf_settings.PVF_VERSION` / `invocation_context.application_version`. Ensure both `/ws/core/get-user-customer-context` `src/pvf/api/app_context_views.py:185` and `/auth-ws/login-and-get-context` `src/pvf/api/app_context_views.py:176` return it (they share builder). Keep `GET /status` `src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py:372` as fallback/telemetry.\n\n2. **Build-time client version injection:** In `client/nuxt.config.ts:16` add `runtimeConfig.public.appVersion` from `package.json` version + `src/config/config_settings.py:13` sync, or Vite `define: { __APP_VERSION__: JSON.stringify(version) }` reading `client/package.json:3` and/or env `APP_VERSION`. At present `client/package.json:2` has no `version` field — add it and keep bumped with `src/config/config_settings.py:13` per `AGENTS.md:139`. Inject at `nuxt build`.\n\n3. **Types & OpenAPI regen:** Update `client/types/api.ts:59` `UserCustomerContextView` interface to match backend. Run `src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py:379` sessions OpenAPI rebuild and regenerate `openapi_sessions.json`/`openapi_api.json`.\n\n4. **Frontend comparison composable:** Create `client/composables/useVersionCheck.ts` (or extend `client/composables/useAuth.ts:19`). Logic: `clientVersion = useRuntimeConfig().public.appVersion`; `serverVersion = context.value?.server_version ?? context.value?.settings?.server_version`; `mismatch = !!clientVersion && !!serverVersion && clientVersion !== serverVersion`. Strict string equality (no semver parsing). Gracefully handle `undefined/null` → no banner, avoid false positive on dev where versions absent. Optional `console.warn` + telemetry hook.\n\n5. **Banner UI component:** Create `client/components/VersionBanner.vue` — `v-alert type=\"warning\"` yellow (`color=\"warning\"` like `client/layouts/default.vue:164`), prominent near top above `page-container`. Props: `serverVersion`, `clientVersion`. Text: \"A new version is available — reload to update.\" Actions: `Reload` button `window.location.reload()` and `Dismiss` button. Accessible (role=\"alert\", aria-live, focusable button). Non-blocking, not modal. Styled yellow box per brief.\n\n6. **Layout integration & dismissal state:** Insert banner in `client/layouts/default.vue:158` `<v-main>` top (above read-only alert) and optionally `client/layouts/auth.vue`, `client/layouts/share.vue` for share flows. Dismiss per-session via `sessionStorage.setItem('versionBannerDismissed:<serverVersion>', '1')`; re-show on next load or when `serverVersion` changes. Auto-dismiss after reload naturally.\n\n7. **Long-lived session re-check (optional but recommended):** On `window.focus` / `visibilitychange` and/or 5-15m polling call `refreshContext()` `client/composables/useAuth.ts:104` or `apiFetch('/status')` `client/components/AboutDialog.vue:59` and recompute mismatch. Keeps banner current without polling storm.\n\n8. **Tests, cache, telemetry:** Add `src/tests/test_context.py:42` assertion that context contains versions. Add e2e check in `e2e/`. Ensure Vite/Nitro cache-busting via hashed assets (Nuxt default) + no `appManifest: false` masking. If telemetry desired, log mismatch to `log_event` or frontend analytics; don’t block UX.\n\n----- QUESTIONS AND RECOMMENDATIONS -----\n**Q1. Where is active server version stored/retrieved?** Source is `src/config/config_settings.py:13` `GlobalSettings.VERSION` exposed via `pvf_app_runner.py:375` `invocation.application_version || pvf_settings.PVF_VERSION`. Recommendation: Expose same value via extended `get_user_customer_context` (not new service) — reuse existing context endpoint.\n\n**Q2. Reload type — hard vs soft, service-worker/cache clear?** Option A: `location.reload()` (soft). Option B: `location.reload(true)`/ hard + cache header. Option C: unregister SW. Recommendation: `window.location.reload()` only; add hard reload + SW clear only if PWA/cached assets prove sticky (not currently).\n\n**Q3. Dismissible duration?** Option A: permanent `localStorage`. Option B: per-session `sessionStorage`. Option C: snooze timer. Recommendation: Dismissible per session (`sessionStorage` keyed by server version) — solves annoyance without hiding future updates; no permanent hide.\n\n**Open Q4. Also log/telemeter mismatch?** Recommendation: Yes — lightweight `console.warn` + optional `log_event` / analytics event `client_version_stale` for staleness monitoring; no user-facing effect.\n\n**Options & Trade-offs**\n- **Versions in context vs `/status` polling:** Context (recommended) = zero extra request, authenticated, coupled to session; `/status` = unauthenticated polling, extra chat. Trade: context needs model change but cleaner.\n- **Return both versions from server vs client injects own:** Both-from-server simplifies client but couples deploy; Client-injected + server-only (recommended) = truth separation, cache-bust validation, minimal backend change.\n- **Comparison strict equality vs semver:** Strict equality recommended — build tags are opaque, avoids semver false-negatives; semver adds complexity for no benefit.\n- **Banner placement via layout vs plugin:** Layout insertion near top `client/layouts/default.vue:159` recommended — visible on all authenticated pages, accessible, not global overlay; plugin would duplicate.\n\n","time":{"start":1787409699202,"end":1787409745781},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"msg_01a029eb91427973a61fabc2a468f55b"}}}}
